public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;

    //@  requires \exists int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;

    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < \result; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  ensures arr[\result] == elem;
    //@ also

            return -1;

        //@ decreases arr.length - i;

public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}

```

+ You SHOULD only insert the specifications into the source code given to you. You SHOULD NOT modify any content other than the comments inserted into the code.
+ You SHOULD output the code in its entirety, without omitting any original content.

public class RotateMatrix180 {
    //@ requires true;
    //@ ensures (A == null || A.length == 0 || A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0 || 
    //@          (\exists int i; 1 <= i < A.length; A[i] == null || A[i].length != A[0].length)) ==> \result == null;
    //@ ensures (A != null && A.length > 0 && A[0] != null && A[0].length > 0 &&
    //@          (\forall int i; 1 <= i < A.length; A[i] != null && A[i].length == A[0].length)) ==>
    //@         (\result != null &&
    //@          \result.length == A.length &&
    //@          \result[0].length == A[0].length &&
    //@          (\forall int i; 0 <= i < A.length;
    //@            \forall int j; 0 <= j < A[0].length;
    //@              \result[i][j] == A[A.length - 1 - i][A[0].length - 1 - j]));
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i <= m;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int k; 1 <= k < i; A[k] != null && A[k].length == n);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases m - i;
        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= m;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int x; 0 <= x < i; \forall int y; 0 <= y < n; ret[x][y] == A[m - 1 - x][n - 1 - y]);
        //@ loop_writes ret[*][*], i, j;
        //@ decreases m - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            //@ maintaining 0 <= j <= n;
            //@ maintaining (\forall int y; 0 <= y < j; ret[i][y] == A[m - 1 - i][n - 1 - y]);
            //@ decreases n - j;
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}

```
public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}

    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))

    //@ ensures (A == null || A.length == 0 || A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0 || 
    //@          (\exists int i; 1 <= i < A.length; A[i] == null || A[i].length != A[0].length)) ==> \result == null;
    //@ ensures (A != null && A.length > 0 && A[0] != null && A[0].length > 0 &&
    //@          (\forall int i; 1 <= i < A.length; A[i] != null && A[i].length == A[0].length)) ==>
    //@         (\result != null &&
    //@          \result.length == A.length &&
    //@          \result[0].length == A[0].length &&
    //@          (\forall int i; 0 <= i < A.length;
    //@            \forall int j; 0 <= j < A[0].length;
    //@              \result[i][j] == A[A.length - 1 - i][A[0].length - 1 - j]));
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i <= m;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int k; 1 <= k < i; A[k] != null && A[k].length == n);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases m - i;
        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= m;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int x; 0 <= x < i; \forall int y; 0 <= y < n; ret[x][y] == A[m - 1 - x][n - 1 - y]);
        //@ loop_writes ret[*][*], i;
        //@ decreases m - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            //@ maintaining 0 <= j <= n;
            //@ maintaining (\forall int y; 0 <= y < j; ret[i][y] == A[m - 1 - i][n - 1 - y]);
            //@ loop_writes ret[i][*], j;
            //@ decreases n - j;
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class DivArrayByTwo {
    public static void DivArrayByTwo(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null)
            return;

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; ++i) {
            arr[i] = arr[i] / 2;
        }
    }
}

public class DivArrayByTwo {
    //@  requires arr == null;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  assigns arr[*];
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == \old(arr[i]) / 2;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static void DivArrayByTwo(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null)
            return;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] == \old(arr[j]) / 2;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k; i <= k < arr.length; arr[k] == \old(arr[k]);
        //@ loop_writes arr[*], i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;

            arr[i] = arr[i] / 2;
        }
    }

Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.
```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```
Note that:
+ You SHOULD generate `ensures` statements for every method, and `maintaining` statements for every loop.
+ Consider adding `/*@ spec_public @*/` specifications to all the members (especially private ones) within the class.

+ You SHOULD output the code in its entirety, without omitting any original content.

assistant: ```
public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class Find {
    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {
        if (arr == null)
            return -1;

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)
            if (arr[i] == elem)
                return i;
        return -1;
    }
}

```

+ You SHOULD generate `ensures` statements for every method, and `maintaining` statements for every loop.

public class Find {
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  requires \exists int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures 0 <= \result < arr.length;
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < \result; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  ensures arr[\result] == elem;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr == null || \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == -1;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ pure
    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {
        if (arr == null)
            return -1;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] != elem;
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)
            if (arr[i] == elem)
                return i;
        return -1;
    }
}

Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.
```
public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}

```

public class RotateMatrix180 {
    //@ requires true;
    //@ ensures (A == null || A.length == 0 || A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0 || 
    //@          (\exists int i; 1 <= i < A.length; A[i] == null || A[i].length != A[0].length)) ==> \result == null;
    //@ ensures (A != null && A.length > 0 && A[0] != null && A[0].length > 0 &&
    //@          (\forall int i; 1 <= i < A.length; A[i] != null && A[i].length == A[0].length)) ==>

    //@              \result[i][j] == A[A.length - 1 - i][A[0].length - 1 - j]));
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i <= m;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int k; 1 <= k < i; A[k] != null && A[k].length == n);

                return null;

            //@ decreases n - j;
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {

        }

        return ret;
    }

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }

        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i <= m;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int k; 1 <= k < i; A[k] != null && A[k].length == n);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases m - i;
        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= m;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int x; 0 <= x < i; \forall int y; 0 <= y < n; ret[x][y] == A[m - 1 - x][n - 1 - y]);
        //@ loop_writes ret[*][*], i;
        //@ decreases m - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            //@ maintaining 0 <= j <= n;
            //@ maintaining (\forall int y; 0 <= y < j; ret[i][y] == A[m - 1 - i][n - 1 - y]);
            //@ loop_writes ret[i][*], j;
            //@ decreases n - j;
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class RotateMatrix180 {

        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;

    //@ requires true;
    //@ ensures (A == null || A.length == 0 || A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0 ||

    //@ ensures (A != null && A.length > 0 && A[0] != null && A[0].length > 0 &&
    //@          (\forall int i; 1 <= i < A.length; A[i] != null && A[i].length == A[0].length)) ==>
    //@         (\result != null &&
    //@          \result.length == A.length &&
    //@          \result[0].length == A[0].length &&
    //@          \fresh(\result) &&

    //@            \forall int j; 0 <= j < A[0].length;
    //@              \result[i][j] == A[A.length - 1 - i][A[0].length - 1 - j]) &&
    //@          (\forall int i; 0 <= i < A.length; \result[i] != A[i]) &&
    //@          (\forall int i; 0 <= i < A.length; \result[i] != null));
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        //@ maintaining 1 <= i <= m;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int k; 1 <= k < i; A[k] != null && A[k].length == n);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases m - i;
        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= m;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int x; 0 <= x < i; \forall int y; 0 <= y < n; ret[x][y] == A[m - 1 - x][n - 1 - y]);
        //@ loop_writes ret[*][*], i;
        //@ decreases m - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            //@ maintaining 0 <= j <= n;
            //@ maintaining (\forall int y; 0 <= y < j; ret[i][y] == A[m - 1 - i][n - 1 - y]);
            //@ loop_writes ret[i][*], j;
            //@ decreases n - j;
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }

    //@ assigns \nothing;
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

                return null;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= m;
